% after 2.2.23 (T1 G1.2.4-6 M om. lines 1 and 3,

02_020_0007 ayaM pArthAd anavamo dhanurvede parAkrame
02_020_0008 ekalavye sthite rAjan kathaM kRSNas tvayArcitaH
02_020_0009 bhagadatte mahAvIrye jayatsene ca mAgadhe
02_020_0010 kAliGge ca sthite rAjan kathaM kRSNas tvayArcitaH

02_020_0018 ayaM ca sarvarAjJAM yo balazlAghI mahArathaH
02_020_0019 jAmadagnyasya dayitaH ziSyo viprasya bhArata
02_020_0020 yenAtmabalam Azritya jarAsaMdho yudhA jitaH

02_020_0022 vindAnuvindAv Avantyau kAmbhojaM ca sudakSiNam
02_020_0023 sthite sAlve ca rAjendre kathaM kRSNo 'rcitas tvayA
